# netmon test traceability matrix

Generated by `tests/gen_docs.py` from `tests/requirements.py` and the `REQ:` tags in each test's docstring - do not edit by hand. How the tests work: [TESTING.md](TESTING.md).

**323 requirements, 323 covered (100%), 1233 tests (1065 unit, 168 end-to-end).**

Last run: 3 expected failure (known bug), 1230 pass

## Summary

| [AUD-05](#aud-05) | Audit log | No audit entry is lost while Elasticsearch is slow, busy or restarting: deferred (429/503) entries, a full queue and the queue at shutdown go to the disk spool; re-sends are idempotent (_id = event.id); malformed entries are kept in a dead-letter file; entries are dropped only when disk space runs low. | AUDIT_LOG.md (How it works) | 10 | 0 |

| [EVW-04](#evw-04) | Event window | The event reviewer labels a correlated group (connected decided-related links) with ONE call once its newest event is one window plus ingest slack old and none of its links is undecided, storing the label on every member; a labelled group is reviewed again only after it gains a member and settles again. | docs/CORRELATION.md | 3 | 0 |
| [EVW-06](#evw-06) | Event window | The Elasticsearch pull (watcher) runs once per event window, following the setting live, with bookmark windows that touch end to end; a group only settles once a pull has committed arrivals past its newest event plus one window. | docs/CORRELATION.md | 4 | 0 |
| [EVW-07](#evw-07) | Event window | A late Elasticsearch arrival is never dropped (arrival-time pull from the bookmark) and keeps its real event time (alerts.event_ts) for correlation and settling; arriving after its group was labelled it still links within the window by event time and causes exactly one re-review. | docs/CORRELATION.md | 2 | 0 |
| [EVW-08](#evw-08) | Event window | Every open event gets an event reviewer label even after its review run stopped being the newest and when it has no link (a group of its own); until then it shows Pending review (with its settle time, or queued), and an open event that will never be labelled shows 'Not reviewed: <reason>' - never a blank. | docs/CORRELATION.md | 3 | 0 |
| [EVW-05](#evw-05) | Event window | Events whose group has not settled show a filterable 'Pending review' badge with the time it settles (never blank); a high-severity event with no label gets a provisional label at once; the AI review page counts events pending review and the settle-to-label delay. | docs/USER_GUIDE.md | 2 | 0 |

## Requirement → tests

### AUTH-01

**Authentication:** Passwords are stored only as salted scrypt hashes; a wrong password never verifies. _(source: ACCESS_CONTROL.md)_

| Level | Test | Verifies | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| unit | `test_auth.TestHashing.test_hash_format_is_salted_scrypt` | A stored hash has the form scrypt$n$r$p$salt$dk with a 16-byte salt and 32-byte key. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestHashing.test_same_password_gets_different_salts` | Hashing the same password twice gives two different stored values (random salt). | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestHashing.test_verify_accepts_right_and_rejects_wrong_password` | The right password verifies; a wrong, empty or case-changed one does not. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestHashing.test_verify_never_raises_on_damaged_hash` | A malformed stored hash (wrong field count, bad hex, None) simply fails verification. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestLogin.test_login_failures_same_message` | Wrong password and unknown user both fail with the same generic message. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestLogin.test_login_success_records_last_login` | Correct credentials return a valid cookie, no error, and set last_login. | pass |

**Authentication:** Sessions are signed tokens that expire (SESSION_HOURS) and are revoked by logout or a password change. _(source: ACCESS_CONTROL.md)_

| Level | Test | Verifies | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| unit | `test_auth.TestChangeOwnPassword.test_change_success_reissues_cookie` | A valid change returns a new working cookie and revokes the old session. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestLogin.test_login_success_records_last_login` | Correct credentials return a valid cookie, no error, and set last_login. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_cookie_roundtrip` | A freshly made cookie maps back to its user and has four dot-separated fields. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_deleted_user_session_rejected` | A valid signature for a user that no longer exists yields no session. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_expired_cookie_rejected` | A cookie whose expiry lies in the past is refused. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_expiry_uses_session_ttl` | The expiry field is now + SESSION_TTL (SESSION_HOURS, default 12 h). | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_garbage_cookie_values` | Empty, None and non-cookie strings give no user and never raise. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_generation_bump_revokes_sessions` | After the user's gen changes (password change / reset) old cookies stop working. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_short_secret_file_regenerated` | A truncated secret key file is replaced, invalidating cookies signed with the old key. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_signature_is_hmac_of_body_with_secret` | The signature is HMAC-SHA256 over 'b64user.exp.gen' with the secret key, which is owner-only. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestSessions.test_tampered_cookie_rejected` | Changing the user, expiry, gen or signature of a cookie invalidates it. | pass |
| unit | `test_auth.TestUserManagement.test_set_password_by_admin_bumps_generation` | An admin reset (actor != user) needs no current password, changes the hash and bumps gen. | pass |
| e2e | `test_e2e_access_control.UserAdmin.test_delete_user_revokes_access` | Deleting an account ends its existing session. | pass |
| e2e | `test_e2e_dashboard.Account.test_change_own_password_signs_out_other_sessions` | Changing one's password keeps this session, revokes the others and the new password works. | pass |
| e2e | `test_e2e_dashboard.SignIn.test_login_sets_strict_session_cookie` | A successful login sets an HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite=Strict session cookie with a Max-Age. | pass |
| e2e | `test_e2e_dashboard.SignIn.test_logout_clears_cookie` | POST /api/logout expires the cookie in the browser. | pass |
| e2e | `test_e2e_dashboard.SignIn.test_tampered_session_rejected` | A modified session cookie is not accepted. | pass |

### AUD-05

**Audit log:** No audit entry is lost while Elasticsearch is slow, busy or restarting: deferred (429/503) entries, a full queue and the queue at shutdown go to the disk spool; re-sends are idempotent (_id = event.id); malformed entries are kept in a dead-letter file; entries are dropped only when disk space runs low. _(source: AUDIT_LOG.md (How it works))_

| Level | Test | Verifies | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| unit | `test_audit.TestEntries.test_full_queue_spools_without_raising` | When the in-memory queue is full, emit writes the entry to the disk spool instead of dropping or blocking. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_bulk_uses_event_id_as_doc_id` | Entries with an event id are created under that _id, so a re-send after a timeout cannot duplicate them. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_busy_items_are_retried_not_dropped` | 429 / 503 item errors (ES overloaded, shard not ready) raise Retry with exactly those entries. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_chunks_bounded_by_bytes` | Large LLM entries are split into bulk requests of at most BULK_BYTES. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_conflict_counts_as_stored` | A 409 on our own _id means the entry was stored by an earlier attempt: counted as sent, not retried. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_drain_keeps_deferred_and_rest` | A drain chunk with deferred entries keeps them plus every later spooled entry, nothing else. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_drops_only_when_disk_low` | The spool refuses entries only when free disk space falls below SPOOL_MIN_FREE. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_flush_spools_deferred_entries` | flush spools entries ES deferred and keeps the ones it stored out of the spool. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_malformed_entries_go_to_dead_letter` | Entries rejected as malformed (400 parsing) are kept in the dead-letter file, not resent or lost. | pass |
| unit | `test_audit.TestNoLoss.test_shutdown_spools_queue` | On shutdown the entries still in memory are written to the spool. | pass |

### STO-14

**Store:** A person's Expected / Benign verdict on an event can name the artifacts it applies to (scope: device, kind of finding, IDS signature, IPs, DNS names, processes, accounts, ports); any event showing all of them gets that verdict. ### CFG-05

**Configuration:** The watcher and the review job select events by arrival time (event.ingested; @timestamp for documents without it) with a per-job bookmark, so events that reach Elasticsearch late are checked when they arrive: watcher windows touch end to end, the bookmark moves only after a run without failed checks, dashboard time ranges stay on event time, and per-source ingest lag is measured. _(source: SETUP.md (Collection windows))_

| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_first_run_uses_nominal_span_ending_before_now` | First run: (now - margin - N min, now - margin] on event.ingested; docs without it fall back to @timestamp. | pass |

| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_manual_run_never_commits` | job None (manual collect.py N) neither reads nor writes a bookmark. | pass |
| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_other_spans_cover_the_job_window` | A since() with another span never starts after the job window (e.g. the 60-min DNS-tunnel check). | pass |
| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_review_overlap_keeps_lookback` | overlap=True (review job): from = min(bookmark - back, to - lookback), so its usual lookback overlap stays. | pass |
| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_time_range_stays_event_time` | A dashboard time range (NETMON_WINDOW_END) always selects by @timestamp, even in arrival mode. | pass |
| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_uncommitted_window_is_covered_again` | Without commit (a failed run) the next window starts at the old bookmark again. | pass |
| unit | `test_config.TestArrivalWindow.test_windows_touch_end_to_end` | After commit the next watcher window starts exactly where the last one ended (no gap, no overlap), | pass |
| unit | `test_issues.RawChecksTests.test_ingest_lag_levels` | Log pipeline backlog: none at p99 <= 30 min, medium above, high above 4 h; evidence names each late source. | pass |

### EVT-01

**Event times:** Each event is mapped to the Elasticsearch records behind it (Zeek, Suricata, endpoint, auth logs; most specific query first, then broader ones) and gets their first and last @timestamp inside its window; IDS signatures, admin sessions and gateway events keep their own record times; status findings with no record behind them get none. _(source: USER_GUIDE.md (Open events vs Posture))_

| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_direct_types_need_no_query` | IDS signatures, admin sessions and gateway events already carry record times: copied, nothing queried. | pass |
| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_falls_back_to_broader_query` | When the specific record is not found, the next, broader alternative answers. | pass |
| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_host_finding_process` | A new-process host finding looks up that executable's process events on that host. | pass |
| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_nothing_found_is_none` | No matching record -> (None, None), so the page falls back to when netmon saw it. | pass |
| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_port_spike_uses_device_and_port` | A baseline port spike looks up the device's Zeek connections on that port inside the event's window. | pass |
| unit | `test_evtime.SpecTest.test_status_findings_have_no_record` | Status findings (agent offline, silent device, Kubernetes inventory) have nothing to look up. | pass |

### SET-01

**Settings: Integrations URLs:** Elasticsearch and UniFi URLs must be http:// or https:// without spaces, quotes or brackets; other values are refused and nothing is saved, and accepted URLs are stored without the trailing slash. _(source: SETUP.md (Integrations))_

|---|---|---|---|
| unit | `test_settings_integrations.UrlSettings.test_bad_urls_refused` | Elasticsearch and UniFi URLs that are not http(s) or contain spaces/quotes/brackets are refused and nothing is saved. | pass |
| unit | `test_settings_integrations.UrlSettings.test_good_urls_normalised` | http:// and https:// URLs are accepted, trimmed and stored without the trailing slash. | pass |
| unit | `test_settings_integrations.UrlSettings.test_http_layer_refuses_other_schemes` | The shared HTTP helper refuses anything but http(s) before opening a connection. | pass |

### SET-02

**Settings: Elasticsearch URL:** The Elasticsearch URL is ES_URL from the environment, else the saved setting, else site.json es_url; a blank setting means the fallback. _(source: SETUP.md (Integrations))_
